# Syndicate — Work Lanes

> Lanes are exclusive by file path. Two lanes may not claim the same file.
> Max concurrent OPEN lanes: 3 (see `state.md`).
> Managed by `/lane`. Do not hand-edit while a session is running.

> **History lives in `lanes_history.md`.** This file is read at the start of
> every session, so it carries each lane's CURRENT state plus one prior block --
> **plus any block that declares file claims**, which `lane-guard` reads from
> here and nowhere else. 36 superseded blocks (2,667 lines) were moved out
> verbatim on 2026-08-18. Nothing was summarised or deleted: if a lane's earlier
> reasoning matters, it is there under the same slug.

**Method note for the next sweep.** `.syndicate/.current-lane.<uuid>` marker
filenames match archived `sessionId`s exactly (6 of 13 did), so a marker whose
id resolves to an ARCHIVED session is hard evidence the lane is orphaned. The
markers for running sessions did NOT match any roster id, so the mapping proves
death, never life — do not invert it.

### nfl-score-clock-model — OPEN — opened 2026-09-27 — session 4ab694ed-003e-4dbe-8966-f39ec57c0b31
- **OWED: RE-GRADE AFTER WEEK 4 `[2026-09-27, user: "re-grade after tonight's games"]`.** The folded-vs-unfolded comparison currently rests on 33 games (weeks 1-3) and CANNOT adjudicate: the home-advantage swing that would explain the unfold's worse result has a 95% CI over games of [-4.939, +1.626], containing both the fitted +1.0 and zero. Tonight's week-4 slate adds ~16 games (33 -> ~49, a ~48% larger sample). **THE TRIGGER IS THE SAME SIGNAL THE HELD DEPLOY WAITS ON** -- the state-based quiet watcher, which must SEE the slate run and then end (confirmed armed 19:08Z: `seen_live=1`). **RUN, in this order:**
  1. `py -3 scripts/fit_nfl_score_clock.py --grade-season 2026 --grade-weeks 1,2,3,4 --model <schema-2 artifact>` — the unfolded arm. It REFUSES if 2026 is in `fit_seasons`, so out-of-sample is asserted not assumed.
  2. The same over weeks 1,2,3,4 against a FOLDED (schema-1) refit, for the paired comparison. Note the loader REFUSES schema 1 by design, so the folded arm needs its own checkout of the pre-`30c4b0b2` fit or an explicit schema-1 path — do NOT relax `SUPPORTED_SCHEMA_VERSIONS` to make it run, that gate exists because folded cells read as unfolded invert a trailing team.
  3. Re-measure mean rest-of-game home advantage for 2026 with its bootstrap CI. If the CI still contains +1.0, the comparison remains unadjudicated and should be SAID so rather than resolved by whichever arm looks better.
  **DO NOT re-grade the smartsim2 re-sim on week 4 expecting a clean read:** there is no week-4 ratings artifact, so every week-4 game rates (0.0, 0.0) and refuses `unfed_ratings`. That refusal is correct and is the reason the reason-split was added; it means the re-sim arm gains nothing from tonight until that artifact is published.
- **UNFOLD BY SIDE: DONE, and it made the grade WORSE. The 2026 sample cannot say whether that is real. 2026-09-27.** Schema 2 drops the leader-mirror and buckets by SIGNED margin in the home-positive frame (`H4-10` / `A4-10` / `TIED`), so home-field advantage lives in the cells instead of being averaged away. Refit on 7 seasons excluding 2020 (1,871 games, 5,613 observations; 2020 excluded because empty stadiums suppressed the very quantity the unfold exists to capture). Graded out of sample on 2026: **worst powered bucket 0.1873 folded -> 0.2188 unfolded**, MAE 8.731 -> 8.834, bias +1.966 -> +2.497. **THE LIKELY MECHANISM IS HOME-ADVANTAGE DRIFT, AND IT IS NOT ESTABLISHED.** Mean rest-of-game home advantage by season: 2023 +1.292, 2024 +1.094, 2025 +0.993, **2026 -1.778** — a ~2.8 point swing that closely matches the +2.497 bias, and which would explain both the unfold hurting (it faithfully encodes a historical advantage that has moved) and the fold accidentally helping (mirroring cancels the home term). But 2026 is 33 games and its 95% CI over games is **[-4.939, +1.626]** — it contains the fitted +1.0 AND zero. **So neither the drift nor the unfold's harm is established; both rest on 33 games.** Schema 2 is KEPT because the defect it fixes is measured on a 272-game season (+2.25 points discarded by the fold) while the evidence against it is not, and the comparison is OWED a re-grade once 2026 has more weeks.
- **TWO DEFECTS FOUND IN MY OWN WORK, both silent, both caught by measurement rather than tests.** (1) `fit()` declared UNSIGNED `margin_buckets` while keying `cells` SIGNED, so the model matched nothing and fell back to the pooled quarter: **86 of 99 graded requests answered from the pool despite only 2 of 27 cells being underpowered**, and every hand-written fixture passed throughout because they supplied signed labels the producer was not emitting. Fixed, and a PRODUCER->CONSUMER round-trip test now drives the real `fit()` and asserts a CELL is selected. (2) The first unfold ran on 3 seasons and pushed 90 of 99 requests below the power floor — halving the cells without adding data quietly removed the margin conditioner altogether.
- **GOAL: PARTLY MET, and the lane's own falsification test FIRED on one clause. 2026-09-27.** Restating the Goal: *beat both the frozen baseline and the smartsim2 re-sim, fitted on prior seasons so the grade is out of sample*. **BEATS THE RE-SIM decisively; does NOT beat FROZEN.** Fitted on 2023-2025 (816 games, 2,448 observations), graded on 2026 which it never saw: worst powered bucket **0.1873 with all 10 buckets powered**, against the re-sim's 0.3185 uncorrected and 0.2410 with rating uncertainty propagated. Dispersion is **0.946 with NO tuning** (re-sim: 0.674 raw, 0.842 corrected). Coverage is complete -- 99 of 99 cutoffs answered from POWERED cells, zero pooled fallbacks and zero refusals, where the re-sim refused 19-43. **But MAE 8.731 vs FROZEN 8.545**, so the falsification clause fired: it loses, by 0.19 where the re-sim loses by 1.93. And 0.1873 is above `#499`'s 0.150, so it does NOT license publishing. Nothing reaches a lens or a pricer.
- **A HARNESS BUG FOUND AND FIXED, and it would have made this whole lane meaningless.** `backtest_nfl_live_totals._fetch_week` sent `year=<season>` to ESPN's NFL scoreboard, which IGNORES that parameter and serves the CURRENT week for any value. Verified: `year=2024&week=1` returned events dated 2026-09-10..13; `dates=2024&week=1` returned 2024-09-06..08. So `--season` was inert for every past season and the FIRST fit silently trained on 33 games of 2026 -- its own test set -- and reported success. Fixed to `dates=`; the refit returned 816 games. The 2026 grade in that harness is unaffected because 2026 IS the current season, so the wrong parameter happened to return the right games.
- **A MODELLING DEFECT MEASURED AND NOT YET FIXED: the sign-fold discards home-field advantage.** The fit mirrors every observation onto the leader's perspective to double its cells, which assumes leading-home and leading-away are mirror images. Measured on 2025: REST is home-positive REGARDLESS of who leads -- home leading +1.076, away leading +1.176, tied -0.011 -- i.e. **+2.252 points of home advantage are folded away**. **NOT CLAIMED: that this explains the graded bias of +1.966.** It points the other way (discarding home advantage should make the model UNDER-favour home, a negative bias), so the sign is unexplained and is the next thing to measure, not a story to attach. Un-folding by side is the obvious next version.
- **NOT CLAIMED, deliberately:** that MAE-vs-frozen is the right test for a DISTRIBUTIONAL model -- frozen has no distribution and cannot price anything -- but the lane wrote that clause before seeing the result and it fired, so it is reported as fired rather than reinterpreted after the fact.
- Goal: an NFL live margin model built from SCORE AND CLOCK alone beats both the frozen baseline and the smartsim2 re-sim on 2026 cutoff-replay, fitted on prior seasons so the grade is out of sample, and its worst powered predicted-probability bucket is reported against 499's 0.150 bar
- Files: scripts/fit_nfl_score_clock.py, syndicate/features/nfl/score_clock.py, tests/test_nfl_score_clock.py
- Hypothesis: The re-sim loses to frozen (MAE 9.596 vs 7.522) because its ratings carry about as much noise as signal, while score and time-remaining carry most of the real information. An empirical rest-of-game margin distribution conditioned on quarter -- and on current margin, since a trailing team's behaviour differs -- should beat both
- Falsification test: If the score-and-clock model does not beat FROZEN out of sample, the information is not in score and clock either and the live product needs a different input entirely, not a different estimator
- Verification: Cutoff replay on 2026 completed games, fitted ONLY on 2023-2025: MAE against frozen and against the re-sim, worst powered bucket with per-bucket n, and a bootstrap-over-games CI on the signed bias
- Blocked by: none

### live-prop-grader-cross-sport — OPEN — opened 2026-09-29 — session 4ab694ed-003e-4dbe-8966-f39ec57c0b31
- Goal: A sport-agnostic live-prop grading harness measures a shipped projection's OWN residual against replay-reconciled official box scores, reproducing WNBA's published bucketed residual EXACTLY from the shared core, and emitting NBA's first measured residual to replace the hand-set sigma table its own source comment flags as not backtested.

Stated precisely: I proved the residual's INPUTS identical, not a side-by-side of the final bucketed table, which is why the equivalence is stronger but not literally the sentence above. NBA's first measured residual is emitted: **67 games, 0 failed reconcile, 43,082 samples**, stdev by minutes-left 5.454 / 3.903 / 2.637 / 1.283, every bucket mean within ±0.11 of zero (unbiased).
- Files: syndicate/features/shared/live_prop_grading.py (NEW), scripts/grade_live_prop_projection.py (NEW), tests/test_live_prop_grading.py (NEW)
- Hypothesis: CONFIRMED. The sport-specific surface is only the ESPN league path and the period lengths; `official_box`, `replay` and `reconcile` are the same computation for WNBA and NBA. NBA needed **zero** NBA-specific code.
- Falsification test: PASSED — see the goal verdict.
- Verification: RAN. (a) WNBA equivalence above. (b) NBA reconciles per player on points/rebounds/assists/threes, `end_elapsed` 48.0; a full slate (2026-01-15) reconciles 7/9 on all four stats, the 2 failures assists-only so the per-stat gate still grades them for the rest. (c) The gate is shown to DISCRIMINATE, not merely pass: the same NBA games through the wrong 10-minute spec drop to 6/20, 3/21, 5/20 on minutes.
- **CAVEAT, carried deliberately:** the backtest anchor is prior-games-in-window, NOT production's published sim. The measured spread is an UPPER bound on the projection's error, not production's interval. A run against published sim anchors is a separate job and is not claimed here.
- Next: sigma calibration check in flight (standardise each residual by the sigma production would have used). Nothing existing was modified — `grade_wnba_live_prop_projection.py` is untouched and remains the reference.
- **SIGMA CALIBRATION MEASURED — NBA's hand-set table is TOO WIDE in all 12 buckets.** Standardising each residual by the sigma production would actually have used (`base * sqrt(remaining/48)`): points POOLED stdev(z) **0.7744** (n=42,057) → correct base **6.97, not 9.0**; rebounds **0.8298** (n=40,364) → **2.90, not 3.5**; assists **0.7074** (n=40,507) → **2.12, not 3.0**. Consequence: NBA live probabilities are systematically UNDER-confident — pulled toward 0.5, so edges are understated and rows under-ranked or withheld.
- **THE ANCHOR CAVEAT CUTS THE SAFE WAY, which is why this verdict survives it.** My anchor is weaker than production's published sim, and a weaker anchor can only WIDEN a residual. Measured is NARROWER than assumed anyway, so 1.29x / 1.21x / 1.41x are LOWER bounds on how too-wide the table is — the DIRECTION of the finding cannot be an artefact of the anchor.
- **SECOND, SMALLER DEFECT: the sqrt SHAPE is also slightly off, and it is distinct from the base.** stdev(z) is not flat across buckets — points 0.692 / 0.765 / 0.788 / 0.777, assists 0.580 / 0.671 / 0.712 / 0.717 — so the law over-widens most in the last five minutes. A scalar base re-fit fixes most of this and NOT that; stated so the next session does not read a corrected base as a fully calibrated model.
- Next: the re-fit is NOT applied — no change to `nba/cards.py` in this lane, and NBA is out of season until late October so nothing is served off it today. Applying it needs the two defects separated and a decision on the shape.

### mlb-live-prop-grader — OPEN — opened 2026-09-29 — session 4ab694ed-003e-4dbe-8966-f39ec57c0b31
- Goal: MLB's live prop re-sim becomes gradeable: a feed_live replay reconciles EXACTLY against the official boxscore, per player, for the stats that are actual prop markets (hits, total bases, home runs, RBIs, runs, batter strikeouts, pitcher strikeouts and outs), over a stated corpus with its date coverage reported.
- Files: syndicate/features/shared/mlb_live_prop_grading.py (NEW), scripts/grade_mlb_live_prop_projection.py (NEW), tests/test_mlb_live_prop_grading.py (NEW)
- Hypothesis: feed_live allPlays carries enough structure to reconstruct every prop-market stat exactly -- result.eventType for the batting line, result.rbi for RBIs, runners[].movement.end=='score' for runs, and matchup.pitcher for pitcher strikeouts -- with no text parsing of play descriptions.
- Falsification test: The replay does NOT reproduce the official boxscore per player for a stat, in which case that stat is not gradeable from allPlays alone and the adapter must say so rather than grade it anyway.
- Verification: Reconcile rate per stat over a sample of feed_live games, reported as a RATE with its denominator and the date window it rests on.
- **GOAL: MET for the replay half.** Corpus **618 games / 47 dates (2026-05-28..07-14)**. Reconcile vs the official box: hits, totalBases, homeRuns, rbi, runs **1.0000** (n=12,970 batters); pitcher strikeOuts and outs **1.0000** (n=5,124); batter strikeOuts **0.9997**. **616/618 games clean on all 8 stats.** Zero unknown eventTypes, zero unreadable files.
- **THE GATE IS SHOWN TO DISCRIMINATE, so 1.0000 is evidence rather than a vacuous comparison.** Three deliberate breaks move it: dropping `double` → hits 0.8485 (+69 unknown events); mis-scoring `home_run` as 1 base → totalBases 0.8718; mis-crediting `walk` as a hit → hits 0.7599. An unknown event also flips `stat_reconciles` False for BATTING while leaving PITCHING True.
- **THE 4 STRIKEOUT MISSES ARE A SOURCE-DATA DEFECT, NOT A GAP IN THE EVENT TABLE.** In both games `matchup.batter.id` disagrees with the play DESCRIPTION — desc "Brandon Lowe strikes out swinging" carries batter id 691373 (Jhostynxon Garcia); desc "Sebastián Rivero called out on strikes" carries 681351 (Logan O'Hoppe). Parsing the description would "fix" these two and make the adapter depend on prose, which the module refuses by design. DECISION: keep the structural read and let the gate refuse those 2 games.
- **COVERAGE INTERSECTION MEASURED BEFORE BUILDING ON IT (CLAUDE.md rule).** feed_live **47** dates, roster_objs **28** (06-15..07-12), daily_summary **0** on disk. `estimate_live` needs a TeamRoster, so the projection half can run on **28 dates, not 47** — and the triple intersection is **0**. Any MC residual rests on the 28, and that is a ceiling, not a starting point.
- **CORPUS PROVENANCE, stated:** the primary tree's on-disk mirror (618 games). Git-tracked is **146 over 11 dates**, and production `/api/ops/artifacts/export` returns the SAME 146 — so this measurement rests on untracked mirror data that production does not currently hold.
- Next: wire `estimate_live` at sample points for the residual, and build the rescale baseline to compare against on the same replayed games. NOT started.
- **PUBLISHING THE CORPUS TO PRODUCTION IS BLOCKED, and the blocker is informative `[user: "get the corpus onto production first"]`.** `/api/ops/artifacts/publish` returns **403 `relative_path is not an allowed hot artifact`**. feed_live matches none of the 200 `HOT_ARTIFACT_PATTERNS`, and crucially **`is_hot()` is False for the 146 files ALREADY on production** — so those never arrived by publish. They are GIT-TRACKED and ship in the deploy checkout. Anything that reads "production has it" as "the publisher put it there" is wrong about this family.
- **BOTH REMAINING ROUTES CARRY A REAL COST, so this is a decision and not a chore.** (a) Adding feed_live to `HOT_ARTIFACT_PATTERNS` also governs PULLS, not just publishes — every worker would pull **78 MB** of historical feed_live on a recurring basis, on services whose documented scarce resource is MEMORY (`#632`), to serve a one-off backtest. (b) Committing the 472 missing files is **61.6 MB** into a tree where `data/` is deliberately a lossy cold-start subset.
- **AND IT IS UNNECESSARY: THE CORPUS IS REPRODUCIBLE, VERIFIED NOT ASSUMED.** `vendor/mlb_bettingv2/tools/datasets/backfill_statsapi_feed_live.py --start-date 2026-05-28 --end-date 2026-07-14 --season 2026` rebuilds it from `statsapi.mlb.com/api/v1.1/game/<pk>/feed/live` — public, unauthenticated, immutable history. Rebuilt 2026-05-28 into a scratch dir: **6/6 MEASUREMENT-identical** (same reconcile counts per stat), **0/6 byte-identical** (gzip timestamps differ, which is why byte-identity is the wrong test).
- **RECOMMENDATION, pending the user's call:** pin the corpus DEFINITION (date range + that one command) rather than ship 78 MB. Production's job is serving the board, not holding replay history, and a reproducible fetch satisfies "the measurement does not rest on untracked data" without a permanent worker cost.
- **PROJECTION HALF: MEASURED. RE-SIM BEATS RESCALE IN ALL SIX STATS, BY ABOUT 1%.** 316 games, **142,050 paired samples**, **120 sims** (production's own count). Pooled |residual| MAE **0.3899 re-sim vs 0.3941 rescale**, diff **-0.0042** CI [-0.0047,-0.0038]. Per stat, all CIs clear of zero: H -0.0034, HR -0.0021, R -0.0045, RBI -0.0050, SO -0.0043, TB -0.0061.
- **STATISTICALLY UNAMBIGUOUS, PRACTICALLY NEGLIGIBLE — and the two must not be reported as one thing.** At n=142,050 the CI is about ±0.0005, so ANY consistent difference clears zero; significance here carries almost no information about SIZE. The relative effect is **0.8-2.1%**, i.e. re-sim moves a projected total-bases figure by ~0.006. That will essentially never flip a side against a line.
- **THE VALIDATION SLICE OVERSTATED THE EFFECT 3x, which is why it was labelled BELIEVED.** 8 games / 60 sims gave pooled diff **-0.0135**; the full 316-game / 120-sim run gives **-0.0042**. A small-sample pilot in this harness is directionally useful and quantitatively unreliable.
- **COST, measured in the same run: 11.8 s/game** for 5 `estimate_live` calls at 120 sims (~2.4 s per live pricing call). So re-sim buys ~1% accuracy for that compute on refresh-worker, per game per tick. Whether that trade is worth it is a decision, not a measurement — but it is NOT the NFL situation, where re-sim LOST outright (MAE 9.596 vs 7.522).
- **WHAT THIS DOES NOT ESTABLISH, stated so it is not over-read.** (1) Both arms are the SAME MC engine, so this compares re-sim against a rescale OF ITSELF — it says nothing about whether a WNBA-style MEASURED-RESIDUAL probability would beat either. (2) **Neither arm has been graded against a PRICE.** This is projection accuracy only, and the NFL prop work already showed a model can project acceptably and still lose to the market on every line. No admission or pricing decision follows from this table.
- **MLB LIVE PROBABILITIES vs THE PRICE: graded, and they are OVERCONFIDENT where it matters `[user: "grade MLB live probabilities against the price"]`.** Graded the SERVED numbers, not a re-run: each `live_lens_report` liveProps row carries `modelProbOver` and the `odds` beside it; outcomes from that game's feed_live box. **584 rows over 7 dates (2026-06-01..06-11)** after drops.
- **THE NULL IS CLEAN, which is what makes the rest readable.** Bet-everything: n=584 hit 0.5411 avg_odds 1.942 **ROI +0.0192 CI [-0.0599,+0.0983]** — indistinguishable from zero. The NFL anytime-TD study died on a null of +16.4%; this one does not, so the game-scoped join (gamePk + name resolved only against THAT game's box) holds.
- **CALIBRATION: the two buckets carrying 75% of the rows are OVERCONFIDENT.** `[0.55,0.7)` n=380 claims **0.6347**, realises **0.5789**, CI [0.5288,0.6276] — the claim sits ABOVE the interval. `[0.7,0.85)` n=56 claims 0.7358, realises **0.5357**. Below 0.55 it is calibrated. So the defect is not everywhere: it is concentrated exactly where the model is confident enough to bet.
- **ROI FALLS MONOTONICALLY AS CLAIMED EV RISES — the same signature as the NFL prop sweep.** EV>0.00 **+0.0060**, >0.05 **-0.0044**, >0.10 **-0.0200**, >0.20 **-0.1224**. No single threshold's CI clears zero (n is small and odds ~1.94), so no one number is established; **the MONOTONE ORDER across four nested thresholds is the finding**, and it says the claimed edge is anti-correlated with realised return.
- **WHAT IS NOT ESTABLISHED, and why no exclusion follows.** n=584 over 7 dates is SMALL and every threshold CI includes zero. Two-sidedness is **0%** (0 of 402 player/line keys carry both sides), so no de-vig and no Brier-vs-market exists at any sample size — this is ROI at the quoted price only. **154 rows (19%) dropped as `pitcher_props`, which names no statistic** (lines 5.5 / 15.0 / 17.5 in one bucket); inferring the stat from the line would fabricate the join key. 42 more dropped on player resolution.
- **THIS IS A CALIBRATION DEFECT, NOT A REASON TO DROP THE MARKET `[user: "every single line is its own entity"]`.** The probability is honest below 0.55 and inflated above it. The actionable read is to re-fit the confident end against this residual, not to withhold MLB live props — and the fix is the same SHAPE as NBA's (whose sigma is too WIDE, i.e. under-confident): both publish an uncertainty nobody measured.
- **RE-FIT ATTEMPTED AND IT IS NOT THE FIX `[user: "refit the confident end against the measured residual"]`.** Gated on the 2026-09-08 rule (DECOMPOSE before prescribing a recalibration; a transform moves RELIABILITY only). Murphy split at 5/8/10/15/20 bins: Brier **0.25407** = reliability **0.0137-0.0214** - resolution **0.0074-0.0164** + uncertainty **0.24831**. Reliability is **5.4-8.4%** of Brier across bin counts, so **a PERFECT recalibration is worth at most ~8.4%** — the same ballpark as the 09-08 case that was correctly called a dead end.
- **THE DECISIVE TEST: the CONFIDENT END LOSES TO A CONSTANT.** Paired, with the constant fitted OUT OF SAMPLE (leave-one-date-out base rate, so the baseline cannot peek at the outcomes it is scored on). ALL rows: model-constant **+0.00394 CI [-0.0059,+0.0138]** — indistinguishable, nothing established. EV>0: **+0.00537 CI [-0.0057,+0.0164]** — indistinguishable. **EV>0.10 (n=334): +0.02140 CI [+0.0050,+0.0378] → LOSES**, the only CI in this study that clears zero.
- **THE TRANSFORM IS A HARM-REDUCER, NOT A REPAIR.** Recalibrated-constant at EV>0.10 is **+0.00276 CI [+0.0008,+0.0047]**: it removes **~87%** of the excess Brier and **still loses to a constant**. The fitted slopes say why — **b = 0.38, 0.08, 0.20, 0.33, 0.28, 0.60, 0.45** across folds, all far below 1.0. It helps by SHRINKING the model's confidence toward the base rate, i.e. by discarding signal, not by correcting a bias. Resolution ~0.01 against uncertainty 0.248 is the same statement: the probability barely discriminates.
- **THE POPULATION BIAS IS AMPLIFIED IN THE TAIL THE BOARD ACTS ON, not absent from it** (the 2026-09-21 rule, firing the dangerous way). Claimed vs realised: ALL **0.5974 / 0.5411** (gap +0.056); EV>0 **0.6145 / 0.5375** (+0.077); EV>0.10 **0.6414 / 0.5150** (**+0.127**).
- **NO `measured_market_skill` ENTRY FILED, deliberately.** That registry encodes skill vs THE MARKET and its `VERDICT_LOSES` is literally `loses_to_market`. **No market probability exists here** — 0 of 402 player/line keys are two-sided, so no de-vig at any sample size — and my comparator was a CONSTANT. Filing the entry would put a claim in the registry that no measurement supports. `PHASE_LIVE` exists and is NOT the blocker; the missing thing is a price to compare against.
- **RECOMMENDATION: do not ship the transform as a fix, and do not call this fixed.** It is defensible as damage limitation IF MLB live props keep pricing off this model, but it does not make the confident end informative. The real repair is the WNBA shape — derive P(over) from a MEASURED residual instead of the sim's own spread — which is a build, not a patch. **And widen the window first: n=334 over ONE 11-day window (06-01..06-11) is thin for a calibration that would touch every live MLB prop.**
- **RETRACTION: "liveProps stopped populating after 2026-06-11" WAS WRONG. It was a MIRROR GAP, not a producer regression `[user: "widen the capture window"]`.** Production holds **238** live_lens_report files over **132 dates (2026-04-10..09-30)**; the local checkout had **110 / 87**. Pulling the 45 missing dates (56.9 MB targeted egress, not the full 120.1 MB) found **28 of them carry liveProps**, including 09-12 **959**, 09-19 **1119**, 09-20 **999**, 09-23 **988**, 09-26 **935**, 09-17 590, 09-13 519, and 09-29 **148** today. The producer is healthy and emitting MORE than in June.
- **THE FAILURE MODE IS SHARPER THAN THE STANDING RULE, and worth naming.** I DID check production — the file COUNT (238 vs 110). Then I diagnosed the liveProps CONTENT pattern from the LOCAL files and reported a 3.5-month silent regression. A production check of one property bought false confidence about a different property. Checking that production HAS more files is not checking WHAT IS IN THEM.
- **Also explains the apparent contradiction I had not resolved:** many liveProps dates show `live: 0` at snapshot time. The rows persist in the report after games finish, so "no game live when the snapshot was taken" never implied "no live props captured" — another reason the local-only read looked like a regression.
- **WINDOW AFTER WIDENING: ~8,100 liveProps rows over ~35 dates** (804 local + ~7,300 pulled), against 584 graded before — roughly a **10x** sample. The binding constraint moved from quotes to OUTCOMES: feed_live covers only 05-28..07-14, so the Aug/Sep rows have no box score yet. Backfilling 08-18..09-29 from the public StatsAPI (free, immutable) is in flight; 04-10 / 05-09 / 05-18 are three isolated dates not yet fetched.
- Pulled reports live at `scratchpad/lens_pull/` (session-local, NOT committed — they are production artifacts, and `live_lens_report_*` IS hot, so a worker can pull them itself).
- **WIDENED RUN: 6,308 graded rows / 32 dates (06-01..09-29), 10.8x the narrow run. IT REVERSES TWO OF THREE CONCLUSIONS `[user: "rerun the price grading on the widened set"]`.** Null stays clean: bet-everything ROI **-0.0345 CI [-0.0676,-0.0014]** (avg_odds 2.852 — the widened set carries far more longshots: homeRuns 993, H+R+R 1,068).
- **REFUTED — "the confident end LOSES to a constant".** That rested on n=334 from one 11-day window. On 6,308 rows the model **BEATS** an out-of-sample constant in EVERY subset, all CIs clear: ALL **-0.03157** [-0.0358,-0.0273]; EV>0 **-0.03507**; EV>0.10 **-0.03758** [-0.0452,-0.0300]; EV>0.20 **-0.02669**. The narrow-window sign was an artefact and is withdrawn.
- **ALSO REFUTED — "the probability barely discriminates".** Decomposition flips: resolution **0.0325-0.0352** (narrow: 0.0074-0.0164) and reliability **0.0032-0.0037 = 1.5-1.7% of Brier** (narrow: 5.4-8.4%). Brier 0.21791 against uncertainty 0.24931. The model has REAL resolution; its error is almost entirely LEVEL.
- **CONFIRMED AND GENERALISED — the overconfidence.** All SIX buckets overconfident, every claim outside its interval, monotone across the whole range: 0.1995/**0.1351**, 0.3862/**0.3158**, 0.5022/**0.4723**, 0.6285/**0.5803**, 0.7543/**0.6841**, 0.9041/**0.7143**. Pooled 0.5294 claimed vs **0.4737** realised, gap **+0.0557**. Not just the confident end — everywhere.
- **AND THE RE-FIT IS NOW THE RIGHT FIX, WHICH REVERSES MY OWN RECOMMENDATION.** Leave-one-date-out, recal-raw: ALL **-0.00257 CI [-0.00389,-0.00125]**, EV>0 **-0.00294**, EV>0.10 **-0.00551 CI [-0.00838,-0.00264]** — helps out of sample in all three, every CI clear. **The fitted SLOPES are the reason it is a different fix than I described: min 0.961 / median 1.002 / max 1.029** (narrow run: 0.08-0.60, median ~0.33). Slope ~1.0 means a pure LEVEL shift — it is NOT shrinking the signal, so my "harm-reducer that works by discarding information" was a small-sample artefact too.
- **WHY THE MODEST BRIER GAIN UNDERSTATES THE VALUE.** The fit buys ~1.2% of Brier, but it removes a **5.6 pp systematic overstatement**, which is what MANUFACTURES phantom edges. ROI stays negative everywhere (ALL -0.0345, EV>0 **-0.0071**, EV>0.10 -0.0163, EV>0.20 -0.0435) and the null is -0.0345, so EV selection does add ~2.7 pp over betting everything while reaching nothing profitable. The correction does not make money appear; it stops edges that were never there being surfaced.

### e2e-coverage-contract — CLOSED — opened 2026-09-29, closed 2026-10-02 — session 4ab694ed-003e-4dbe-8966-f39ec57c0b31 — **GOAL: MET** (verification ran: the contract resolved all 6 active sports from one production payload and the gate named 7 unverifiable cells; deployed and re-measured 5 —> 2 defects). - Files: syndicate/features/shared/coverage_contract.py (NEW), scripts/check_e2e_coverage.py (NEW), tests/test_coverage_contract.py (NEW)
- Hypothesis: The per-sport diagnostic schema is non-uniform (MLB/NHL/soccer use rows_considered/rows_with_projection; NFL/NCAAF/WNBA use game_rows_*/prop_rows_*), and that inconsistency -- not any single sport's data gap -- is why no reliable cross-sport end-to-end view exists.

- Verification: The contract resolves all six active sports from one production payload, records WHICH key it used per cell, and the gate names every unattributed zero -- demonstrated against the live shortlist.
- **GOAL: MET.** The contract resolves all **6** active sports from one production payload, records the key each cell came from, and the gate exits non-zero naming **7** cells that make end-to-end state unverifiable.
- **HYPOTHESIS CONFIRMED, and it had already cost a real misread.** Three DIFFERENT resolution paths are needed: split (`game_rows_*`/`prop_rows_*` — NFL/NCAAF/WNBA), derived (`considered - no_projection` — MLB), and combined (`rows_*` — NHL/soccer). Reading MLB under the split keys it does not emit gave None, and I was one step from reporting MLB's healthy **773/882 = 87.6%** as **0%**. Falsification did NOT trigger: no single key set resolves every sport.
- **MLB, THE REFERENCE MODULE, WAS THE LEAST LEGIBLE ROW UNTIL THE DERIVATION.** Before: games fell through to a COMBINED games+props 763/871 and props read `not_reported`. After: **games 241/245 (98.4%), props 587/626 (93.8%)** from MLB's own `game_no_projection` / `player_no_projection`.
- **THE 7 REMAINING DEFECTS ARE FINDINGS, not gate noise.** 4 x `unattributed_zero` on `live_games` (ncaaf, nfl, nhl, wnba) — `rows_live_gameline_projected: 0` with **no reason**, so a code gap and a Monday slate are indistinguishable. 3 x `not_reported`: `nhl.pregame_props`, `soccer.pregame_props`, `soccer.live_games` — those sports emit no such metric at all.
- **WHAT THE GATE DELIBERATELY DOES NOT FAIL ON**, so it stays adoptable rather than red-forever: an `attributed_zero` ("no live re-sim wired for nhl", "no soccer match in play") is a KNOWN gap tracked in a lane, not an instrument defect; and `degraded` partial coverage passes, because a per-sport healthy floor has never been measured and inventing one here would encode a guess as a threshold.
- Verification RAN against the live shortlist (written_at 22:38:38Z). 16 offline tests, every absence case covered: absent key → `not_reported` never zero, blank reason → unattributed, bool never coerced to a count, active sport with no ingest block still appears as a row.

The 834 is dominated by ~459 player-prop rows a full-game join was never meant to touch, and they were INVISIBLE to the denominator rather than refused-and-counted. Two diagnoses went to the join and the artifact read before the counter was found; neither was at fault.

Without it `prop_rows_considered` would span the whole multi-date grid while `rows_considered` covered one date — two populations in one payload, the exact defect this lane removes.

(2) A half's `reason` frozen at date one is served as the window's; reasons are now collected, deduped, and pruned AFTER the loop, kept only when that half's summed projection is actually zero. Distinct refusals stay distinct rather than collapsing into one.

Falsification did not trigger — no reason was being set on that path.
- **THREE DISTINGUISHABLE STATES, because they have three different owners.** Nothing in the snapshot → "no <sport> game in play in the published live snapshot" (nobody at fault). Games present, none indexed → "16 nfl game(s) in the live snapshot but none indexed (skipped_no_accepted_lane=16)" (the producer or the accepted-source list, and the skip buckets say which). Indexed but no row matched → "3 ncaaf game(s) indexed from 5 in the snapshot, but no board row matched them" (the row/game join, NOT the producer).
- **IT REFUSES TO TOUCH A WORKING JOIN.** A join that CONSIDERED rows already explains itself through `withheld_by_reason`, so writing a coarse summary over that would lose the detail. Non-numeric counters mean the state is unknown, so it says nothing rather than guessing — and never crashes the enrichment step.
- **SECOND, SMALLER FIX IN THE SAME PASS: `rows_live_gameline_projected: 0` added to all FOUR early returns.** They emitted only `rows_live_gameline_edged`, and the contract reads `projected` — so soccer's `live_games` read `not_reported` (invisible, schema drift) even though its reason string had been correct all along. An attributed zero needs BOTH the count and the reason to be present.
- Tests: 11 new, covering all three states, the skip-bucket filtering, an existing reason never being overwritten, malformed/absent diagnostics, and the two integrations (gate stops reporting the unattributed zero; a working join still reads as real coverage). **563 board_enrichment/live_gameline/coverage_contract tests pass.**
- **NOT YET VERIFIED ON PRODUCTION** — needs a refresh-worker deploy. The reading that settles it: `scripts/check_e2e_coverage.py` reporting 0 `unattributed_zero` cells, down from the 3 live_games zeros, and soccer's `live_games` moving off `not_reported`.
- Claim history: `board_enrichment.py` was held by lane `nfl-live-resim-activation`, which its own block already recorded as GOAL: MET. CLOSED it (a met lane left OPEN is the drift the protocol warns about) and verified with the guard's own `lane_claims._claims` that this lane then held the file alone.
- **THE MISS IS FIXED IN CODE (2026-09-30, not yet deployed), and it was a DESIGN error of mine, not an incomplete rollout.** The first version returned early whenever `rows_live_gameline_considered > 0`, on the reasoning that such a join "already explains itself through `withheld_by_reason`". Right about the DATA, wrong about the READER: the contract reads `reason` and cannot see `withheld_by_reason`, so NHL shipped `considered: 9, withheld_by_reason: {no_live_gameline_projection: 9}` and still measured `unattributed_zero` on production. Now only a NON-ZERO `projected` returns early.
- **MY STATED WORRY DID NOT HOLD.** I avoided this branch because "a coarse summary would lose the detail" — but the summary goes in `reason` ALONGSIDE `withheld_by_reason`, which is left untouched. There was never a trade-off.
- **THE TEST SUITE HAD THE SAME BLIND SPOT AS THE CODE, which is the more useful lesson.** Both "left alone" tests used a NON-ZERO `projected`, so they passed on the early-return branch and NOTHING exercised `projected == 0` with `considered > 0`. 11 tests passed and the defect still reached production. 6 new tests close it, and I verified by simulation that the NHL-shape test FAILS against the old logic — a test that cannot fail against the bug it is written for proves nothing.
- Ranked and truncated: reasons are ordered by count with a `+N more` tail so a long breakdown is summarised rather than silently cut. Rows withheld with NO breakdown recorded is itself NAMED, because that is a gap in the join's own accounting and must not read as a plain zero.
- 17 tests in this file; **569** board_enrichment/live_gameline/coverage_contract tests pass.
- Blocked by: none

### coverage-gate-inactive-sports — OPEN — opened 2026-10-02 — session 4ab694ed-003e-4dbe-8966-f39ec57c0b31
- Goal: check_e2e_coverage stops counting out-of-season sports as defects: a sport present in per_sport_ingest but ABSENT from active_sports is still SHOWN in the matrix and marked inactive, but contributes no defect, so the gate's failure list is only cells that are genuinely unverifiable -- demonstrated on the live fleet where 8 of 9 defects are mlb and nba out of season.
- Files: syndicate/features/shared/coverage_contract.py, scripts/check_e2e_coverage.py, tests/test_coverage_contract.py
- Hypothesis: read_shortlist takes the UNION of per_sport_ingest and active_sports so an active sport cannot vanish from the matrix; that guard is one-directional, so an INACTIVE sport carrying a zero-activity ingest block gets judged and yields 4 not_reported defects each.
- Falsification test: If the fleet's mlb/nba defects come from something other than their absence from active_sports, this hypothesis is wrong and the union is not the cause.
- Verification: SYNDICATE_BASE_URL=http://127.0.0.1:10000 scripts/check_e2e_coverage.py reports 1 defect (soccer.pregame_props) instead of 9, with mlb and nba still VISIBLE as inactive rows, and an ACTIVE sport missing its ingest block still fails.
- **GOAL: MET, verified on the LIVE FLEET (which is production since 09-30/10-01, not Render).** `SYNDICATE_BASE_URL=http://127.0.0.1:10000 scripts/check_e2e_coverage.py` now reports **1 defect instead of 9** — only `soccer.pregame_props`, the genuine one. mlb and nba still appear, marked `(off)`, with every cell visible.
- **HYPOTHESIS CONFIRMED.** `read_shortlist` unioned `per_sport_ingest` with `active_sports` so an ACTIVE sport can never vanish; the guard was one-directional, so an INACTIVE sport carrying a zero-activity block (`grid_rows: 0, opportunities: 0, quote_rows: 0, scheduled_games: 0`) got judged and produced 4 `not_reported` defects each. 8 of 9 = **89% noise**, which is how a gate earns being switched off. Falsification did not trigger.
- **`active` IS THREE-WAY ON PURPOSE.** True (listed) → judged; False (a listing exists and omits it) → shown but exempt; **None (no `active_sports` at all) → STILL JUDGED**. An absent listing does not prove a sport is out of season, and mapping that unknown onto the permissive branch is the exact failure this contract exists to refuse — so the fix does not repeat the defect it is fixing.
- **The exemption is PRINTED, not silent:** "not counted (absent from active_sports, out of season): mlb, nba". A clean verdict that hides what it excluded is unfalsifiable.
- 8 new tests, 23 in the file, 60 across the four consumer files. **Falsification checked: 6 of the new tests FAIL against the pre-fix contract** — a test that cannot fail against the bug it was written for proves nothing.
- Note on my own process: my first version of the headline test asserted `all_defects(matrix) == []`, which was asserting the FIXTURE (the nfl stub supplies only `game_rows_*`, so nfl's other cells are legitimately not_reported). Narrowed to the mlb prefix. Same over-assertion I made in `layer2-window-merge-counters`.
- Blocked by: none

- **FIT RESULT 2026-10-03 ~16:00Z: GOAL: NOT MET -- the PREMISE IS FALSIFIED; no production file edited, nothing to deploy.** The pre-registered Brier bar was cleared (receptions 72.5%, receiving_yards 71.1% of the 2025 gap; no market worse). The pre-registered SLOPE caveat (< 0.6 = shrinking toward the base rate, NOT a repair) was triggered: the PRODUCTION model probability's calibration slope at the line is ~0 in all six markets on 2025 (receptions 0.11, receiving_yards -0.07, rushing_yards -0.01, passing_yards -0.06), against the book's ~1. The winning fits ran to the flattening edge (K off, c=3.0, w=0). The model's probability carries no information at the line; widening only flattens noise. This overturns recommendation #1 of `findings_2026-10-02_nfl_lines_props_backtest.md` section 7 (marked there). Detail: `findings_2026-10-03_nfl_prop_predictive_spread.md`. **BLOCKED on a user decision**: close this lane, or re-scope it to the MEAN's inputs (the market-anchored ceiling, 86-97%), or ship an explicitly-labelled "honest" flattening (per line, no edge).
- Goal: NFL prop probabilities for the continuous markets (receptions, receiving_yards, rushing_yards, rushing_attempts, passing_yards, passing_attempts) are priced from a predictive spread that carries the uncertainty of the rate estimate, re-fitted per model_engine_standard.md (spread-shrinkage k and _COVER_PROBABILITY_BLEND_WEIGHT re-fit on top of it, fit 2023-24), and on held-out 2025 the served-path Brier gap to the de-vigged book closes by at least half for receptions and receiving_yards (lane nfl-lines-props-backtest measured 56-74% for a fitted scale), with no market getting worse; then deployed and read on the fleet's prop artifact. Every line stays shown (user decision: every line is its own decision)

- Hypothesis: The prop loss is reliability (Murphy reliability 10-300x the book's, resolution at or above it): the probability at the line treats the season-to-date rate as known. A predictive sd = sqrt(sd_game^2 + var(rate)), with var(rate) fitted per market, removes most of it without touching the mean

- **PREDICTION, written BEFORE the fit ran (2026-10-03 ~04:40Z):** `scripts/fit_nfl_prop_predictive_spread.py`, fit 2023-24, scored on 2025 + 2026 wk2. For receptions and receiving_yards the chosen arm is `drift`/`drift_refit` with c in 1.2-2.5 (a flat x2.5 at the line implies c*mean/sd ~ 2.3, and mean/sd ~ 1.4 for receptions). It closes >= 50% of the 2025 gap with a calibration slope within 0.8-1.2. For rushing/passing volume the drift arm closes 20-40% (the mean, not the spread, is their binding problem). If the slope comes out < 0.6, the fit is shrinking toward the base rate, not fixing the spread, and the result is NOT a repair.
- **RELEASED TO THIS LANE 2026-10-03** by `layer2-triad-alignment` (session 4ab694ed, asked not overridden, `133be4f4`): the NFL prop model files, now on the `- Files:` line above. Its author also SUPERSEDES its own 2026-09-29 eight-market vs-book result (`log/2026-09-29.md`, the source of the NFL `VERDICT_LOSES` entries in `measured_market_skill.py`): that run built rates via `backtest_nfl_props._rate_from_log`, which omits production's zero-week imputation, so it graded an estimator production does not serve. The CLOSED lane `nfl-lines-props-backtest` called `player_rate` directly (selfcheck 0/8,976 vs `nfl_props_rows_for_week`), so its numbers are the served model and are this lane's baseline.

### wnba-prop-dispersion — OPEN — opened 2026-10-02 — session 39b666bb-e708-4edf-bfe5-3dda419e3589
- **Goal (verbatim): "WNBA SmartSim prop ladders are calibrated in width: a per-market dispersion estimator, fit on early-season as-of re-run ladders and tested out of sample (Aug-Sep regular season + playoffs), moves each priced market's ladder P(over) Brier at the book line toward the book with a paired game-clustered CI, keeps line-level reliability flat across the published ladder (not only interval coverage), and does not move the mean; then wired into the engine WNBA-only behind a flag with an off!=on reachability test. No deploy without a user decision" -- GOAL: MET for the measurement and the wiring; NOT ENABLED anywhere (user decision owed).** Out of sample (train May-Jul, test Aug-Sep + playoffs, 5,233 player-games/market): interior k per market (pts 1.35, reb 1.25, ast 1.25, threes 1.15, PRA 1.6, PR 1.5, PA 1.4, RA 1.3); held-out book-line Brier improves on 7 of 8 markets with CIs < 0 (pts -0.0181 [-0.0214,-0.0151], PRA -0.0372), whole-ladder RPS too; threes inert by rounding. Still behind the book (+0.035..+0.054): at the book line the sim has NO discrimination for pts/reb/PRA (observed over-rate flat 0.42-0.57 across every predicted decile). Engine: commit "wnba prop dispersion: widen SmartSim prop ladders" (on main), one hook, WNBA-only, flag `SYNDICATE_WNBA_PROP_DISPERSION` default OFF; reachability through the real engine (OFF sd ratio 1.00, ON pts 1.30) and cards_sim_detail + `_hit_prob_over` (P(over mean+8.5) 0.034 -> 0.069). Owed for enablement: allowlist line in artifact_publisher.py (claimed by OPEN lane nhl-live-resim -> cross-lane, needs user approval), factor file on the fleet disk, flag on the props workers, fleet ff. Detail: `findings_2026-10-03_wnba_prop_dispersion.md`.
- **USER DECISION 2026-10-03: HOLD -- do not enable** ("hold it, start on fix #2 minutes"). Flag stays OFF, no factor file on the fleet, no allowlist edit. Re-fit k after the minutes fix (lane `wnba-sim-availability`) before any enablement: a better mean leaves less residual for the width to absorb.
- Goal: WNBA SmartSim prop ladders are calibrated in width: a per-market dispersion estimator, fit on early-season as-of re-run ladders and tested out of sample (Aug-Sep regular season + playoffs), moves each priced market's ladder P(over) Brier at the book line toward the book with a paired game-clustered CI, keeps line-level reliability flat across the published ladder (not only interval coverage), and does not move the mean; then wired into the engine WNBA-only behind a flag with an off!=on reachability test. No deploy without a user decision
- Files: scripts/fit_wnba_prop_dispersion.py (NEW), tests/test_fit_wnba_prop_dispersion.py (NEW), .syndicate/findings_2026-10-03_wnba_prop_dispersion.md (NEW), syndicate/features/shared/wnba_prop_dispersion.py (NEW), tests/test_wnba_prop_dispersion.py (NEW)
- **CLAIM RELEASED 2026-10-03:** `syndicate/features/shared/basketball_props_smart_sim.py` -- this lane's one hook is landed (commit "wnba prop dispersion: widen SmartSim prop ladders"); the file passes to lane `wnba-sim-availability` (same session) for its own one-call hook.
- Hypothesis: Measured 2026-10-02 (lane wnba-lines-props-backtest): sim residual sd / sim sd = 1.39 pts, 1.41 reb, 1.45 ast, 1.23 threes, 1.58 PRA; 80% coverage 58-73%; ladder Brier worse than book by +0.013..+0.089. Combos are built from joint draws, so their extra under-dispersion is a missing SHARED (game/minutes-level) variance, not independence. A per-market dilation around the sim mean, k ~ 1.2-1.6, closes most of the Brier gap toward own-average-normal; it will not pass the book
- Falsification test: If the k fit on May-July does not improve held-out Aug-Sep/playoff Brier at the book line with a paired CI excluding 0, or the fitted k sits at a grid edge, or reliability across ladder thresholds gets worse, widening is not the fix and the mean (minutes) must come first
- Verification: Offline: per-market table train/test n, fitted k, held-out Brier model vs book vs own-avg-normal with paired CI, reliability by predicted-probability decile over book lines AND over all ladder thresholds; engine: off!=on reachability test and a re-run date reproducing the offline ladder
- Blocked by: none

No deploy without a user decision

- Verification: Offline table train/test: non-player minutes removed, real players wrongly dropped (count and their actual minutes), played-player minutes bias/MAE with CI, then props re-scored; engine: off!=on reachability test on a re-run date

## 2026-08-17 - THE LEDGER IS A RECORD, NOT EVIDENCE (the inverse of the same day's other lesson)

I relayed *"two uncommitted soccer fixes at risk of being lost"* to the
coordinator as an action item. **It came from a lane entry, not from a
measurement.** `git status` was empty and fix #1 was already on main.

**This is the exact inverse of the three errors recorded above it today.** There
I called healthy things BROKEN from a null lookup. Here I called a committed
thing AT RISK from a written claim I never checked. **Same root cause: treating
a statement as a reading.**

`.syndicate/**` records what was true WHEN WRITTEN. This lane was last touched
two days before I quoted it. **Before acting on or forwarding a ledger claim
about the state of the working tree - uncommitted work, missing files, a broken
service - re-measure it.** The cost here was small (a wrong action item, since
retracted). The cost of the reverse - deleting or "rescuing" files on a stale
claim - would not have been.
